   1++6C3E              ; ============================================================================
   2++6C3E              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6C3E              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6C3E              ;
   5++6C3E              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6C3E              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6C3E              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6C3E              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6C3E              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6C3E              ; ============================================================================
  11++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6C3E              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6C3E
  18++6C3E              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6C3E                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6C3E 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6C41 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6C44 B7                   or      a
  23++6C45 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6C47 C2 86 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6C4A
  26++6C4A                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6C4A 21 99 86             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6C4D 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6C50 B7                   or      a
  30++6C51 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6C53 DA 86 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6C56 CA 86 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6C59 22 A0 6C             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6C5C 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6C5D FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6C5F D2 86 6C             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6C62
  38++6C62 21 99 86             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6C65 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6C68 B7                   or      a
  41++6C69 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6C6B DA 74 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6C6E CA 74 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6C71 C3 86 6C             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6C74              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6C74                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6C74 3A 83 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6C77 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6C79 C2 86 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6C7C 3A 80 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6C7F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6C81 C2 86 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6C84 B7                   or      a
  54++6C85 C9                   ret
  55++6C86
  56++6C86              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6C86 37                   scf
  58++6C87 C9                   ret
  59++6C88
  60++6C88              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6C88              p127_layout_report:

  70++6CA0

 487+ 6CDA              ; Keep the remap-critical code/data at the start of the Stage2 image so it
 488+ 6CDA              ; remains inside W1/P5. The preflight below verifies the actual linked bounds.
 489+ 6CDA                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6CDA              ; ============================================================================
   2++6CDA              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6CDA              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6CDA              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6CDA              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6CDA              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6CDA              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6CDA              ; ============================================================================
   9++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6CDA
  13++6CDA              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6CDA              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6CDA              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6CDA              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6CDA              p127_resident_start:
  18++6CDA
  19++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6CDA              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6CDA
  26++6CDA              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6CDA              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6CDA              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6CDA              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6CDA              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6CDA 32 64 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6CDD 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6CDE 32 65 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6CE1 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6CE2 32 66 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6CE5 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6CE6 32 67 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6CE9
  39++6CE9 3A 64 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6CEC FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6CEE C2 18 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6CF1 3A 65 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6CF4 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6CF6 C2 18 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6CF9 3A 66 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6CFC FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6CFE C2 18 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6D01 3A 67 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6D04 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6D06 CA 18 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6D09                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6D09 3A 64 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6D0C FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6D0E CA 18 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6D11 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6D13 CA 18 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6D16 B7                   or      a
  58++6D17 C9                   ret
  59++6D18
  60++6D18              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6D18 37                   scf
  62++6D19 C9                   ret
  63++6D1A
  64++6D1A              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6D1A              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6D1A              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6D1A 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6D1C 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6D1E 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6D20 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6D22 CD DA 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6D25 DA 62 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6D28
  74++6D28 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6D2A 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6D2C 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6D2E 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6D30 CD DA 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6D33 D2 62 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6D36
  81++6D36 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6D38 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6D3A 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6D3C 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6D3E CD DA 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6D41 D2 62 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6D44
  88++6D44 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6D46 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6D48 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6D4A 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6D4C CD DA 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6D4F D2 62 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6D52

  98++6D58 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6D5A CD DA 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6D5D D2 62 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail

 118++6D68              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.

 248++6E4C              ; Save/restore the complete software W3 ownership tuple.
 249++6E4C              ; Snapshot and rollback are RAM-only; they never touch page registers.
 250++6E4C              p127_w3_snapshot:

 252++6E4F 32 08 6F             ld      (p127_w3_saved_state),a

 258++6E5F C9                   ret

 261++6E60 3A 08 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_state)
 262++6E63 32 05 6F             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 263++6E66 3A 09 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_owner)
 264++6E69 32 06 6F             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a

 266++6E6F 32 07 6F             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 267++6E72 B7                   or      a
 268++6E73 C9                   ret

 270++6E74              ; Recovery tests: reject a competing acquire, preserve a busy UI when
 271++6E74              ; quiesce is refused, roll back a partial HANDOFF acquire, reject a
 272++6E74              ; transition from HANDOFF, then resume and prove UI admission works again.
 273++6E74              p127_w3_recovery_selftest:

 349++6F0B              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6F0B              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6F0B              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6F0B              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6F0B              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6F0B              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6F0B              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++6F0B              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:

 368++6F1F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6F21 D2 58 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 375++6F2B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++6F2D DA 58 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++6F30

 380++6F36 B7                   or      a
 381++6F37 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++6F39 D2 58 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 385++6F3C                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.

   1++6F5A              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F5A              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6F5A              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6F5A              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6F5A              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6F5A              ; ============================================================================
   7++6F5A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6F5A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6F5A              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6F5A              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6F5A              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6F5A              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6F5A
  14++6F5A              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6F5A              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6F5A              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6F5A              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6F5A              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6F5A              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6F5A              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6F5A              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6F5A              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6F5A              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6F5A              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6F5A
  26++6F5A              p127_irq_init:
  27++6F5A F3                   di
  28++6F5B 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6F5D 32 3C 72             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6F60 AF                   xor     a
  31++6F61 32 3D 72             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6F64 32 3E 72             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6F67 32 3F 72             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6F6A B7                   or      a
  35++6F6B C9                   ret
  36++6F6C

  38++6F6C              p127_irq_validate:

  78++6FBF              ; and resident handler are all explicitly committed. This routine NEVER EI.

   1++7240              ; ============================================================================
   2++7240              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7240              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7240              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7240              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7240              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7240              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7240              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7240              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7240              ; ============================================================================
  11++7240              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7240              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7240              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  14++7240              P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT    EQU 64
  15++7240              P127_MEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  16++7240              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++7240              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1
  18++7240              P127_MEM_ALLOCATED     EQU 2

   1++73BE              ; ============================================================================
   2++73BE              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++73BE              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++73BE              ; ABI:
   5++73BE              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++73BE              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++73BE              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++73BE              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++73BE              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++73BE              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++73BE              ; ============================================================================
  12++73BE              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++73BE              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++73BE              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++73BE              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++73BE
  17++73BE              p127_range_validate:
  18++73BE                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++73BE 21 CC 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++73C1 11 D0 74             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++73C4 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++73C5 47                   ld      b,a
  23++73C6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++73C7 80                   add     a,b
  25++73C8 32 D4 74             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++73CB 23                   inc     hl
  27++73CC 13                   inc     de
  28++73CD 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++73CE 47                   ld      b,a
  30++73CF 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++73D0 88                   adc     a,b
  32++73D1 32 D5 74             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++73D4 23                   inc     hl
  34++73D5 13                   inc     de
  35++73D6 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++73D7 47                   ld      b,a
  37++73D8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++73D9 88                   adc     a,b
  39++73DA 32 D6 74             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++73DD 23                   inc     hl
  41++73DE 13                   inc     de
  42++73DF 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++73E0 47                   ld      b,a
  44++73E1 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++73E2 88                   adc     a,b
  46++73E3 32 D7 74             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++73E6 D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++73E7
  49++73E7                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++73E7 3A D7 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++73EA B7                   or      a
  52++73EB C2 09 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++73EE 3A D6 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++73F1 FE 10                cp      010h
  55++73F3 DA 07 74             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++73F6 C2 09 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++73F9 3A D5 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++73FC B7                   or      a
  59++73FD C2 09 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++7400 3A D4 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++7403 B7                   or      a
  62++7404 C2 09 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++7407              p127_range_valid:
  64++7407 B7                   or      a
  65++7408 C9                   ret
  66++7409              p127_range_invalid:
  67++7409 37                   scf
  68++740A C9                   ret
  69++740B
  70++740B              ; Regression tests exercise the 1 MiB boundary, zero length and uint32 wrap.
  71++740B              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failed assertion.

# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++74D8              ; ============================================================================
   2++74D8              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++74D8              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++74D8              ;
   5++74D8              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++74D8              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++74D8              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++74D8              ;
   9++74D8              ; API:
  10++74D8              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++74D8              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++74D8              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++74D8              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++74D8              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++74D8              ;
  16++74D8              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++74D8              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++74D8              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++74D8              ; ============================================================================
  20++74D8              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++74D8              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++74D8              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++74D8              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++74D8              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++74D8
  26++74D8              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++74D8              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++74D8              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++74D8              p127_arena_init:
  30++74D8 21 BD 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++74DB 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++74DD 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++74DF              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++74DF 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++74E0 B7                   or      a
  36++74E1 C2 EB 74             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++74E4 2B                   dec     hl
  38++74E5 0D                   dec     c
  39++74E6 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++74E8 C3 9E 75             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++74EB              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++74EB 0C                   inc     c
  43++74EC 79                   ld      a,c
  44++74ED FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++74EF D2 9E 75             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++74F2 32 2F 76             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++74F5 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++74F7 91                   sub     c
  49++74F8 CA 9E 75             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++74FB 32 30 76             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++74FE 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++7500 32 31 76             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7503 B7                   or      a
  54++7504 C9                   ret
  55++7505
  56++7505              ; IN B=non-zero page count. OUT A=first page of the new block.
  57++7505              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.
  58++7505              p127_arena_alloc:

   1++7636              ; ============================================================================
   2++7636              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7636              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7636              ;
   5++7636              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7636              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7636              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7636              ;
   9++7636              ; API:
  10++7636              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7636              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7636              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7636              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7636              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7636              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7636              ; ============================================================================
  17++7636              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7636              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7636              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++7636              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++7636              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++7636              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++7636
  24++7636              p127_as_init:
  25++7636 21 D7 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++7639 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++763B 11 D8 77             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++763E 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++7641 ED B0                ldir
  30++7643 B7                   or      a
  31++7644 C9                   ret
  32++7645
  33++7645              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.
  34++7645              p127_as_bind:
  35++7645 32 D2 77             ld      (p127_as_logical_temp),a
  36++7648 78                   ld      a,b
  37++7649 FE 40                cp      P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT
  38++764B D2 E9 76             jp      nc,p127_as_reject
  39++764E 32 D3 77             ld      (p127_as_physical_temp),a
  40++7651 5F                   ld      e,a
  41++7652 16 00                ld      d,0
  42++7654 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  43++7657 19                   add     hl,de
  44++7658 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++7659 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
  46++765B C2 E9 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  47++765E
  48++765E 3A D2 77             ld      a,(p127_as_logical_temp)
  49++7661 5F                   ld      e,a
  50++7662 16 00                ld      d,0
  51++7664 21 D7 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  52++7667 19                   add     hl,de
  53++7668 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  54++7669 FE FF                cp      P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  55++766B C2 E9 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  56++766E
  57++766E 21 D7 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  58++7671 06 00                ld      b,0
  59++7673              .p127_as_bind_scan:
  60++7673 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  61++7674 FE FF                cp      P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  62++7676 28 08                jr      z,.p127_as_bind_next
  63++7678 4F                   ld      c,a
  64++7679 3A D3 77             ld      a,(p127_as_physical_temp)
  65++767C B9                   cp      c
  66++767D CA E9 76             jp      z,p127_as_reject
  67++7680              .p127_as_bind_next:
  68++7680 23                   inc     hl
  69++7681 10 F0                djnz    .p127_as_bind_scan
  70++7683
  71++7683 3A D2 77             ld      a,(p127_as_logical_temp)
  72++7686 5F                   ld      e,a
  73++7687 16 00                ld      d,0
  74++7689 21 D7 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  75++768C 19                   add     hl,de
  76++768D 3A D3 77             ld      a,(p127_as_physical_temp)
  77++7690 77                   ld      (hl),a
  78++7691 B7                   or      a
  79++7692 C9                   ret
  80++7693
  81++7693              ; Remove one logical mapping. Does not release the physical page.
  82++7693              p127_as_unbind:

  84++7694 16 00                ld      d,0
  85++7696 21 D7 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table

   1++7AB7              ; ============================================================================
   2++7AB7              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7AB7              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7AB7              ;
   5++7AB7              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7AB7              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7AB7              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7AB7              ;
   9++7AB7              ; API inputs:
  10++7AB7              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7AB7              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7AB7              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7AB7              ; Outputs:
  14++7AB7              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7AB7              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7AB7              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7AB7              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7AB7              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7AB7              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7AB7              ; ============================================================================
  21++7AB7              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7AB7              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7AB7              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

 177++8165              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++8165              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++8165              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++8165              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++8165              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++8165              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++8165              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++8165              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++8165              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++8165                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++8165                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++8165 5F                   ld      e,a

 190++8167 32 26 82             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 191++816A 78                   ld      a,b
 192++816B FE 40                cp      64

 194++8170 7B                   ld      a,e

 210++818F C9                   ret

 216++8194 C9                   ret

   4++835B              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded

  20++835B              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.

  23++835B              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++835B              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++835B              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++835B              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

   1++83F6              ; ============================================================================
   2++83F6              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++83F6              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++83F6              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++83F6              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++83F6              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++83F6              ; ============================================================================
   8++83F6              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9++83F6              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  10++83F6              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2

  12++83F6              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++83F6              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++83F6              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++83F6              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h
  16++83F6              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_COUNT EQU 128
  17++83F6              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  18++83F6
  19++83F6              ; Public entry. Returns carry set on sector-read error.
  20++83F6              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++83F6              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++83F6              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.3
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6
  10+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 80
  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12
  13+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE7 EQU #78
  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25
  19+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_FRAME_SIZE EQU 4000
  20+ A000              P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT EQU #00EF
  21+ A000
  22+ A000 06           p126_atm_video_mode: db P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0

  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0

  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 239+ A149              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 240+ A149              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 241+ A149              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 241+ A149
 242+ A149 F5                   push    af
 243+ A14A C5                   push    bc
 244+ A14B D5                   push    de
 245+ A14C E5                   push    hl
 246+ A14D CD 78 6D             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 247+ A150 DA B9 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 248+ A153                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 249+ A153                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 250+ A153 E1                   pop     hl
 251+ A154 D1                   pop     de
 252+ A155 C1                   pop     bc
 253+ A156 F1                   pop     af
 254+ A157 F5                   push    af
 255+ A158 C5                   push    bc
 256+ A159 D5                   push    de
 257+ A15A E5                   push    hl
 258+ A15B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 259+ A15E 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 260+ A161 79                   ld      a, c
 261+ A162 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 262+ A165              .p126_print_at_loop:
 263+ A165 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 264+ A168 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 265+ A169 B7                   or      a
 266+ A16A CA A6 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 267+ A16D 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 268+ A170 23                   inc     hl
 269+ A171 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 270+ A174 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 271+ A177 57                   ld      d, a
 272+ A178 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 273+ A17B 5F                   ld      e, a
 274+ A17C 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 275+ A17F F5                   push    af
 276+ A180 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 277+ A183 ED 79                out     (c),a
 278+ A185 F1                   pop     af
 279+ A186 CD 5D A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 280+ A189 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 281+ A18C 3C                   inc     a
 282+ A18D FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 283+ A18F DA A0 A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 284+ A192 AF                   xor     a
 285+ A193 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 286+ A196 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 287+ A199 3C                   inc     a
 288+ A19A 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 289+ A19D C3 65 A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 290+ A1A0              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 291+ A1A0 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 292+ A1A3 C3 65 A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 293+ A1A6              .p126_print_at_done:
 294+ A1A6 CD 93 6D             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 295+ A1A9 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 296+ A1AC 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 297+ A1AE ED 79                out     (c),a
 298+ A1B0 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 299+ A1B2 ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A1B4 E1                   pop     hl
 301+ A1B5 D1                   pop     de
 302+ A1B6 C1                   pop     bc
 303+ A1B7 F1                   pop     af
 304+ A1B8 C9                   ret
 305+ A1B9              .p126_print_at_denied:
 306+ A1B9 E1                   pop     hl

# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A3F2              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A3F2              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A3F2              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A3F2              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A3F2              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A3F2
  15+ A3F2              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A3F2              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A3F2              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A3F2              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A3F2              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A3F2              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A3F2              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A3F2 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A3F4 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A3F7 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A3F8 32 63 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A3FB 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A3FE 32 65 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A401 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A404 32 67 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A407 32 68 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A40A
  32+ A40A                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A40A 3A 32 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A40D B7                   or      a
  35+ A40E C2 A5 A6             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state
  36+ A411

  50+ A433

  63+ A44C DA AF A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A44F 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A451 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A454
  67+ A454                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A454 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A457 32 6C AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A45A 2F                   cpl
  71+ A45B 32 6D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_probe),a
  72+ A45E 3E 01                ld      a,1
  73+ A460 32 68 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  74+ A463
  75+ A463                      ; Select allocated page A and write one complemented probe byte.
  76+ A463 3A 69 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  77+ A466 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  78+ A469 DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  79+ A46C 3E 01                ld      a,1
  80+ A46E 32 67 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  81+ A471 3A 6D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_probe)
  82+ A474 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
  83+ A477
  84+ A477                      ; Return to P0. Its saved byte must be unchanged if the OUT worked.
  85+ A477 AF                   xor     a
  86+ A478 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  87+ A47B DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

  96+ A48F DA B4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 224+ A5DE                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.

 226+ A5DE                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.

 266+ A624                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.

 293+ A659                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A659                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.

 297+ A65D 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A65F 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A662 19                   add     hl,de

 316+ A684 3A 70 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A687                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A687 B7                   or      a
 319+ A688 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A68A 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A68D              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:

 323+ A68E 32 70 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a

 372+ A6DC 3A 67 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 397+ A70D
 398+ A70D              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:

 419+ A734 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A736              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A736                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A736 3A 63 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)

 440+ A760 B7                   or      a
 441+ A761 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A763                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A763                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A763                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 447+ A767 C9                   ret
 448+ A768              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A768 37                   scf
 450+ A769 C9                   ret
 451+ A76A
 452+ A76A              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A76A              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A76A              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A76A                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A76A                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A76A                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A76A                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A76A CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init
 460+ A76D
 461+ A76D                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A76D                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 511+ A7D6
 512+ A7D6                      ; Put the decisive result in the fixed status panel as well as the
 513+ A7D6                      ; scrolling log. PrintAt addresses rows directly, so this does not
 514+ A7D6                      ; depend on the current scrolling cursor after the long page replay.

 536+ A7F6              ; A=page ID. Emit two hexadecimal digits through the visible UI console.
 537+ A7F6              p127_physmem_screen_hex8:

 570+ A89F              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:

 573+ A8A4 37                   scf
 574+ A8A5 C9                   ret
 575+ A8A6
 576+ A8A6              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8A6              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8A6              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8A6 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8A9 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8AB 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8AD              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8AD 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8AE FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8B0 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8B2 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8B4 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8B6 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8B8 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8BA FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8BC 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8BE FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8C0 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8C2 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8C4 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8C6 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8C8 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8CA FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8CC 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8CE 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8D0              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8D0 C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8D1 E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8D2 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8D3 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8D5 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8D8 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8DA FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8DC E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8DD C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8DE 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8E0              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8E0 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8E1 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8E3 B7                   or      a
 617+ A8E4 C9                   ret
 618+ A8E5              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8E5 37                   scf
 620+ A8E6 C9                   ret
 621+ A8E7
 622+ A8E7              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A8E7              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A8E7 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A8EA 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A8EB 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A8EE              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A8EE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A8EF 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A8F0 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A8F2 C5                   push    bc
 632+ A8F3 E5                   push    hl
 633+ A8F4 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A8F7 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A8F8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A8F9 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A8FB E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A8FC C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A8FD 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A8FF 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A900 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A902 B7                   or      a
 643+ A903 C9                   ret
 644+ A904              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A904 37                   scf
 646+ A905 C9                   ret
 647+ A906
 648+ A906              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A906              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A906              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A906 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A909 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A90B 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A90D              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A90D 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A90E FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A910 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A912 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A914 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A916 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A918 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A91A FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A91C 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A91E FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A920 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A922 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A924 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A926 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A928 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A92A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A92B B7                   or      a
 672+ A92C 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A92E 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A930              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A930 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A931 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A933 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A935              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A935 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A936 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A937 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A939 B7                   or      a
 683+ A93A C9                   ret
 684+ A93B              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A93B 37                   scf
 686+ A93C C9                   ret
 687+ A93D
 688+ A93D              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A93D              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A93D
 691+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A93D              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A93D              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A93D              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A93D              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A93D              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A93D              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A93D              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A93D              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A93D              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A93D              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A93D C3 BD 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A940
 705+ A940              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A940 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A943 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A946 B7                   or      a
 709+ A947 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A94A 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A94D 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A94E 3A 32 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A951 B8                   cp      b
 714+ A952 CA FA A9             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A955 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A958 C3 D3 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A95B
 718+ A95B              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A95B              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A95B F3                   di
 721+ A95C 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A95F B7                   or      a
 723+ A960 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A963 3A 32 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A966 B7                   or      a
 726+ A967 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A96A CD 98 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A96D D8                   ret     c
 729+ A96E 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A970 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A973 CD B7 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A976 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A978 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A97A 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A97D 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A980 32 9C AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A983 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A985 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A988 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A98B B7                   or      a
 741+ A98C C9                   ret
 742+ A98D              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A98D CD DF 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A990 AF                   xor     a
 745+ A991 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A994 37                   scf
 747+ A995 C9                   ret
 748+ A996
 749+ A996              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A996              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A996 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A999 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A99C FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A99E C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9A1 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9A4 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9A6 D2 FA A9             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9A9 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9AA 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9AC 21 7E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9AF 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9B0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9B1 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9B3 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9B6 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9B9 C3 26 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9BC
 768+ A9BC              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9BC              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9BC              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9BC 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9BF FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9C1 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9C4 AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9C5 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9C8 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9C9 3A 9B AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9CC B7                   or      a
 779+ A9CD 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9CF 3A 9C AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9D2 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9D5              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9D5 3A 9A AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9D8 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9D9 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9DB CD CB 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9DE D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9DF AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9E0 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9E3              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9E3 3A 99 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 793+ A9E7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ A9E9 CD DF 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ A9EC D8                   ret     c
 796+ A9ED AF                   xor     a
 797+ A9EE 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ A9F1              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ A9F1 AF                   xor     a
 800+ A9F2 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ A9F5 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ A9F8 B7                   or      a
 803+ A9F9 C9                   ret
 804+ A9FA
 805+ A9FA              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ A9FA 37                   scf
 807+ A9FB C9                   ret
 808+ A9FC
 809+ A9FC              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ A9FC              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ A9FC              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ A9FC AF                   xor     a
 813+ A9FD 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA00 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA03 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA06 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA09 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA0B CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA0E D2 96 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA11 CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA14 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA17 32 9E AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA1A 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA1D 32 9F AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA20 CD 5B A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA23 DA 90 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA26                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA26 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA28 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA2B D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA2E 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA31 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA34 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA37                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA37 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA3A CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA3D D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA40 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA42 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA45 CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA48 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA4B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA4E 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA4F 3A 9F AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA52 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA53 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA56 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA59 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA5C DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA5F                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA5F CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA62 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA65 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA66 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA69 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA6A C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA6D 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA6E CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA71 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA74 3A 05 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA77 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA79 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA7C 3A 06 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA7F FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA81 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA84 3A 32 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AA87 B7                   or      a
 868+ AA88 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AA8B B7                   or      a
 870+ AA8C C9                   ret
 871+ AA8D
 872+ AA8D              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AA8D CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AA90              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AA90 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AA93 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AA96              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AA96 37                   scf
 879+ AA97 C9                   ret

 890+ AAA0              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AAA0 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AAA3 ED 79                out     (c),a

 894+ AAA6
 895+ AAA6              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAA6              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAA6              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAA6              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAA6 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAA7 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAA8 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAA9 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAAA FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAAC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAAE 21 F8 AA             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AAB1 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AAB3              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AAB3 21 06 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAB6              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAB6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAB7 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAB8 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AABA 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AABB E5                   push    hl
 915+ AABC CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AABF E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AAC0 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AAC2              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AAC2 3A 6F AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AAC5 CD D7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAC8 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AACA CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AACD 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AACF CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char

 927+ AAD4 C1                   pop     bc

 959+ AB0A 46 59 20 50

 962+ AB26
 963+ AB26              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB26              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.

 973+ AB38 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB39 C9                   ret
 975+ AB3A
 976+ AB3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB3A              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB3A              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB40              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB40 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB41 07                   rlca
 984+ AB42 AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB43 AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB44 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AB45 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AB46 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB47 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB48 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB49 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB4B C9                   ret
 993+ AB4C
 994+ AB4C              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB4C              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AB4C 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AB4F 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AB52              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AB52 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AB53 07                   rlca
1001+ AB54 AC                   xor     h
1002+ AB55 AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB56 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AB57 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AB59 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AB5A 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AB5B 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AB5C B1                   or      c
1009+ AB5D 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AB5F B7                   or      a
1011+ AB60 C9                   ret
1012+ AB61              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AB61 37                   scf
1014+ AB62 C9                   ret
